# Dependencies

Generated by `scripts/deps.py` from the facts `scripts/Audit.lean --deps` writes; the gate
regenerates every file and fails if one differs (CONTRIBUTING.md). Do not edit by hand. The graphs
are `docs/deps/{packages,modules,claims}.dot`, rendered to `.svg` and `.pdf` with graphviz `dot`.

## Packages

Every package of `lake-manifest.json` with its exact revision; an edge is a `require` of the
package's lakefile. Loaded: how many of its modules the audit loads (our library and tests and
everything they import); a package with none is required but not imported by MojoProof.

## Claims

The claims are the theorems among the audit targets (`scripts/targets.txt`). Each claim is
walked by `closure` (scripts/Audit.lean): the constants its statement and proof mention,
then those of every declaration of ours they reach (type and body), stopping at the first
external constant. Ours: the declarations of `MojoProof/` reached, auto-generated ones
(matchers, equation lemmas, constructors, projections) not listed. External: the external
constants reached, by package. Axioms: every axiom reached through the whole environment.
TRUST: the ids of docs/TRUST.md whose anchor in `scripts/trust.tsv` the walk reaches. The
complete lists are in `docs/deps/claims.tsv` (claim, kind, module or package, name).

TRUST anchors (`scripts/trust.tsv`): T1 all; T2 module `MojoProof.Kernels`; T3 module `MojoProof.Kernels`; T4 module `MojoProof.LLVM.Lexer`; T4 module `MojoProof.LLVM.Printer`; T4 module `MojoProof.LLVM.Quote`; T4 module `MojoProof.Kernels`; T5 module `MojoProof.LLVM.Intrinsic`; T6 module `MojoProof.Util.SHA256`; T6 module `MojoProof.Kernels`; T7 modules `MojoProof.Sem`; T8 const `MojoProof.Sem.Layout.align`; T9 const `MojoProof.Sem.contract`; T10 const `MojoProof.Sem.rsqrtOk`; T11 const `MojoProof.Sem.Mem.alloc`; T12 const `MojoProof.Sem.withAttrs`; T12 const `MojoProof.LLVM.Func.check`; T12 const `MojoProof.Sem.Grid.Kept`; T13 const `MojoProof.Sem.Grid.Step`.
